## TRAPS CARRIED (B 108th to B 128th HANDOVERs and faults, abbreviated; read the originals)
(1) A typed count is a guess until `grep -o | wc -l` prints it; a PR body's size in BYTES is not its character count (multi-byte characters): count characters; dedupe a transcript count by tool_use id. (2) Check every queued PR title against the merge guard BEFORE the body edit. (3) Add-only PR: the add/add variant of `item1_single`. (4) The builder's hex guard rejects 12+ hex-looking runs: no `aaaa` fillers; wants a FULL 40-hex in an evidence file for every 12-hex token in a CFG note; never quote a mail id there. (5) `body_edit` in BOTH make_cfg and make_mcfg; say the body edit ONCE, in the NEXT file. (6) The counted shift mislabels `matcher_checks` and the controls-section template, and garbles PAIRS, EXTEND and the lineage gap: count with `grep -c` before an asserted replacement. (7) The sender refuses a READY if a MINE mail is newer than your LASTREAD. (8) Reading a 25 to 28 KB draft costs about 7K tokens. (9) `go_regex_test` needs four arguments. (10) Progress lines go under a `### Progress — Seat B 129th` heading before the vault commit. (11) Describe an OLD sentence in a section WITHOUT quoting it verbatim (the edit tool refuses it). (12) zsh: no word starting with `=`, never a bare run of equals signs as a word, arrays for lists, `set -- $var` does not split, `/usr/bin/grep` with a positive control beside every zero; `grep -c` exits 1 on zero and stops a `&&` chain; `UNION=` before the GO; an UNQUOTED heredoc eats backtick spans (quoted heredoc or a script file); no PIPESTATUS.
(13) No `cd` (not even a subshell; the hook refuses it), absolute paths, `GIT_OPTIONAL_LOCKS=0` on every git call in the shared checkout, check a pid's FULL command line before killing, no `rm`, no `find` over 5_Project_History, NO listing of `4_Credentials` and no `.env` variable name printed, the AgentMail key never in curl's argv, `grep -o` with `{0,700}` exceeds macOS's 255 limit, no whole-file rewrite of the shared daily note for one phrase.
